# MX — Mexico sights: individual worldwide reviews

[Worldwide v2](../../../rules.md) · [Future-country workflow](../../future-country-workflow.md)

Initial inventory only. [UNESCO country list](https://whc.unesco.org/en/statesparties/mx/) (accessed 2026-11-25) lists 36 inscribed and 23 tentative properties. Inscribed properties represented in whole or by named component: Mexico City/Xochimilco, Oaxaca/Monte Albán, Guanajuato/mines, Puebla, Morelia, Zacatecas, Campeche, Querétaro, Tlacotalpan, San Miguel/Atotonilco, Cabañas, Teotihuacán, Chichén Itzá, Palenque, Uxmal, Calakmul, El Tajín, Xochicalco, Paquimé, Monarch reserve, Sian Ka'an, El Vizcaíno (22 of 36). Outstanding 14: Agave Landscape of Tequila, Padre Tembleque Aqueduct, Camino Real de Tierra Adentro, UNAM central campus, Popocatépetl monasteries, Sierra Gorda missions, Luis Barragán house, Yagul/Mitla prehistoric caves, Sierra de San Francisco rock paintings, Wixárika sacred route, Revillagigedo, Pinacate/Gran Desierto, Gulf of California islands/protected areas (Cabo Pulmo not a substitute for whole serial nomination), Tehuacán-Cuicatlán. Tentative Izamal represented in cities; other 22 nominations not individually inventoried. See the official list for exact scopes. No sensitive sacred route coordinates or artificial aggregate scores.

All scores are provisional editorial judgments; no independent fact-check status claimed. GPS approximate, not navigation guarantees. Current safety/access not established. Further major standalone destinations and serial components need research.

| Field | Rating | Individual justification |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Impact | 9.7 | vast pyramids and planned avenue |
| Uniqueness | 9.6 | exceptional monumental Mesoamerican city |
| Significance | 9.7 | major ancient political and religious centre |

| Depth | 9.5 | multiple monumental complexes |

- Approved 10 registry: none.
- Final decision: Provisional intrinsic editorial worldwide score for named scope only.

At the preceding review, three of fifteen Popocatépetl monastery components were individually rated. Site-specific visitor scope/earthquake restoration still to be verified; no aggregate score for the serial property.
